## Clef Practice

Practice writing your clef symbol on this staff. Write at least eight clef sym

Write the letter names of the lines in your staff:

<!-- Abbildung (nicht übernommen): PDF-Dateien-konvertiert/documents/b78a30736cc91a0e/d3f6f9dfb2b4/00011-00020/try-20261114T154748-726317/document_artifacts/image_000008_fb64e95fc61f4911aed8ad853d1d7b3ff99eb65122b1b4bf459c3a0de10bd7b4.png -->

Write your clef symbol at the beginning of this line. Then write the correct letter name above each note
